( printed page 37534)
表V.9显示了N-336表格费用调整的经济影响。受N-336表格费用上涨影响的非军事外国人N-336表格的预计收件量将介于4,690份[136](2019财年至2023财年的年平均数)与3,377份[137](2024年4月至2025年3月的年度数)之间;这两个时期之间的中位数为4,034份。[138] 现行全额付费申请人向美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)就N-336表格支付的预计年度转移支付将介于3,025,050美元[139]与2,178,165美元[140]之间;预计年度转移支付中位数为2,601,930美元(主要估计值)。[141]
Table V.9 shows the economic impacts of fee adjustments for Form N-336. The estimated number of receipts of Form N-336 from non-military aliens who would be impacted by the fee increase to Form N-336 would range between 4,690 [136] (annual average, FY 2019 through FY 2023) and 3,377 [137] (annual, April 2024 through March 2025); the median between these two periods would be 4,034.[138] The estimated annual transfer payments from current full fee-paying applicants to USCIS for Form N-336 would range between $3,025,050 [139] and $2,178,165; [140] the estimated median annual transfer payment would be $2,601,930 (primary estimate).[141]
如表V.9所示,军人及退伍军人外国人N-336表格的预计收件量将介于58份(2019财年至2023财年的年平均数)与63份(2024年4月至2025年3月的年度数)之间;[142] 这两个时期之间的中位数为61份。国防部(DOW)不就军人及退伍军人外国人的N-336表格费用向USCIS报销,因此该转移支付将由付费申请人向USCIS支付。现行全额付费申请人向USCIS就N-336表格支付的预计年度转移支付将介于37,410美元与40,635美元之间;预计年度转移支付中位数为39,345美元(主要估计值)。
As shown in Table V.9, the estimated number of receipts of Form N-336 from military members and veteran aliens would range between 58 (annual average, FY 2019 through FY 2023) and 63 (annual, April 2024 through March 2025); [142] the median between these two periods is 61. The DOW does not reimburse USCIS for the fee from military members and veteran aliens for Form N-336, therefore the transfer payment would be from fee-paying applicants to USCIS. The estimated annual transfer payments from current full fee-paying applicants to USCIS for Form N-336 would range between $37,410 and $40,635; the estimated median annual transfer payment would be $39,345 (primary estimate).
受N-336表格费用上涨影响的外国人N-336表格的预计总收件量将介于4,748份[143](2019财年至2023财年的年平均数)与3,440份[144](2024年4月至2025年3月的年度数)之间;这两个时期之间的中位数为4,095份。[145] 综合非军事和军事外国人计算,现行全额付费申请人向USCIS就N-336表格支付的总预计转移支付将介于3,062,460美元[146]与2,218,800美元[147]之间;预计转移支付中位数为2,641,275美元。[148]
The estimated total receipts of Form N-336 from aliens who would be impacted by the fee increase to Form N-336 would range between 4,748 [143] (annual average, FY 2019 through FY 2023) and 3,440 [144] (annual, April 2024 through March 2025); the median between these two periods would be 4,095.[145] The total estimated transfer payments from current full fee-paying applicants to USCIS accounting for non-military and military aliens for Form N-336 would range between $3,062,460 [146] and $2,218,800; [147] the estimated median transfer payment would be $2,641,275.[148]
(印刷页第37535页)
( printed page 37535)
国土安全部(DHS)已提议对N-400表格和N-336表格的审理和处理费用进行调整,以使表格费用能够全额收回提供这些服务的成本,以及执行USCIS不断加强的筛查和审查工作的成本,从而使USCIS能够提高美国入籍制度的完整性,并确保完全遵守现行入籍法律。DHS承认,提高费用可能会对部分申请人申请入籍福利的能力产生不利影响。然而,正如本规则其他部分所述,DHS认为入籍福利属于缺乏弹性的需求,大多数外国人最终都会支付该费用,和/或仅有部分人会短暂推迟提出申请。DHS没有任何数据表明拟议的费用变更会阻止许多外国人申请入籍。
DHS has proposed fees for the adjudication and processing of Form N-400 and Form N-336 so that the form fees would recover the full cost of providing those services, as well as performing screening and vetting checks which USCIS is continuously enhancing, allowing USCIS to improve the integrity of the U.S. naturalization system and ensure full compliance with current naturalization laws. DHS acknowledges that increasing fees might adversely affect some applicants' ability to apply for naturalization benefits. Yet, as described elsewhere in this rule, DHS believes that a naturalization benefit is an inelastic good and that most aliens would ultimately pay the fees and/or only some would briefly defer applying for a request. DHS does not have any data indicating that the proposed fee changes would deter many aliens from applying for naturalization.
5. 修正
5. Amendments
a. 费用减免
a. Fee Waivers
DHS提议修订8 CFR 106.3(a)(3),取消申请N-400表格和N-336表格的外国人获得费用减免的资格。目前,外国人可以请求费用减免。外国人通过满足以下至少一项标准来证明其无力支付费用:
DHS proposes to amend 8 CFR 106.3(a)(3) to end the availability of fee waivers for aliens applying for Form N-400 and Form N-336. Currently an alien can request a fee waiver. An alien establishes an inability to pay the fee by meeting at least one of the following criteria:
家庭收入达到或低于联邦贫困指南(FPG)的150%;[149]
Had a household income at or below 150 percent of the FPG; [149]
该外国人或符合条件的家庭成员目前正在接受经济状况调查类福利;
The alien or a qualifying household family member currently receives a means-tested benefit;
正经历严重的经济困难,例如意外的医疗账单或紧急情况。[150]
Is experiencing extreme financial hardship, such as unexpected medical bills or emergencies.[150]
通过本规则,DHS打算要求申请N-400表格和N-336表格的外国人支付全额拟议费用,而不论其支付能力如何。[151] DHS将继续为根据《移民与国籍法》(INA)第328条或第329条(8 U.S.C. 1439、1440)就兵役申请的外国人提供费用豁免,因为这些费用豁免系法律所要求,且国防部为这些申请提供部分资助。
With this rule, DHS intends to require aliens applying for Form N-400 and Form N-336 to pay the full proposed fee regardless of their ability to pay the fee.[151] DHS would continue to provide fee exemptions for aliens applying under sections 328 or 329 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1439, 1440, with respect to military service, as these fee exemptions are required by statute and DOW provides partial funding for these applications.
受取消费用减免影响的人群将包括随同福利申请(N-400表格或N-336表格)一并请求费用减免的外国人。DHS使用已批准的费用减免请求数据(表V.4和表V.6)来估计拟议取消费用减免对受影响人群的影响。[152] 经济影响按时间段(2019财年至2023财年以及2024年4月至2025年3月)的人群数量进行估算,然后使用两个时期人群数量之间的中位数来计算主要估计值。[153]
The populations impacted by the elimination of fee waivers would include aliens who would request a fee waiver that accompanies the benefit request (Form N-400 or Form N-336). DHS uses approved fee waiver requests (Table V.4 and Table V.6) to estimate the impact on populations affected by the proposed elimination of fee waivers.[152] Economic impacts are estimated using populations by time period, FY 2019 through FY 2023 and April 2024 through March 2025, then a median between the populations of two periods is used to calculate a primary estimate.[153]
表V.10显示了取消N-400表格和N-336表格费用减免的经济影响。美国国土安全部(DHS)估计,受取消费用减免影响的非军籍外国人已批准的N-400表格费用减免数量将介于136,314件(2019财年至2023财年的年平均数)和157,488件(2024年4月至2025年3月的年度数)之间;[154]这两个时间段之间的中位数为146,901件。目前符合非军籍费用减免资格的外国人向美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)就N-400表格支付的估计年度转移支付金额将介于181,297,620美元和209,459,040美元之间;估计的中位数(printed page 37536)年度转移支付金额为195,378,330美元(主要估计值)。
Table V.10 shows the economic impacts of the elimination of fee waivers for Form N-400 and Form N-336. DHS estimates that the number of approved fee waivers for Form N-400 from non-military aliens who would be impacted by the elimination of fee waivers would range between 136,314 (annual average, FY 2019 through FY 2023) and 157,488 (annual, April 2024 through March 2025); [154] the median between these two time periods would be 146,901. The estimated annual transfer payments from current non-military fee-waiver eligible aliens to USCIS for Form N-400 would range between $181,297,620 and $209,459,040; the estimated median ( printed page 37536) annual transfer payment would be $195,378,330 (primary estimate).
如表V.10所示,受取消费用减免影响的非军籍外国人已批准的N-336表格费用减免数量估计将介于794件(2019财年至2023财年的年平均数)和527件(2024年4月至2025年3月的年度数)之间;[155]这两个时间段之间的中位数为661件。目前符合非军籍费用减免资格的外国人向USCIS就N-336表格支付的估计年度转移支付金额将介于1,171,150美元和777,325美元之间;估计的中位数年度转移支付金额为974,975美元(主要估计值)。
As shown in Table V.10, the estimated number of approved fee waivers for Form N-336 from non-military aliens who would be impacted by the elimination of fee waivers would range between 794 (annual average, FY 2019 through FY 2023) and 527 (annual, April 2024 through March 2025); [155] the median between these two time periods would be 661. The estimated annual transfer payments from current non-military fee-waiver eligible aliens to USCIS for Form N-336 would range between $1,171,150 and $777,325; the estimated median annual transfer payment would be $974,975 (primary estimate).
外国人可以通过提交I-912表格或书面请求来申请费用减免。[156]由于DHS拟取消N-400表格和N-336表格的费用减免资格,将不再需要填写I-912表格所花费的时间。DHS估计填写I-912表格的时间负担为1.095小时。[157]机会成本的节省将使不再具备费用减免资格的非军籍外国人受益。
Aliens can request a fee waiver by submitting Form I-912 or by a written request.[156] Because DHS proposes to eliminate eligibility for fee waivers for Form N-400 and Form N-336, there would no longer be a time burden for completing Form I-912. DHS estimates the time burden for completing Form I-912 is 1.095 hours.[157] The opportunity cost of time would be a cost savings for non-military aliens who are no longer eligible for fee waivers.
由于获批费用减免的外国人已证明其无力支付费用,我们预计其中大多数人不会获得“高层级”工资。目前联邦最低工资为每小时7.25美元,[158]但许多州已实施更高的最低工资标准。[159]然而,联邦政府并未追踪全国人口加权最低工资的估计数据。相关群体中的外国人可能分布于美国境内任何地方,并可能根据其所居住的州或城市而适用不同的最低工资标准。
Because aliens approved for fee waivers would have demonstrated an inability to pay the fee, we would not expect many of them to earn “high-tier” wages. The Federal minimum wage is currently $7.25 per hour,[158] but many States have implemented higher minimum wage rates.[159] However, the Federal Government does not track a nationwide population-weighted minimum wage estimate. Aliens in the populations of interest could be located anywhere within the United States and may be subject to a range of minimum wage rates depending on the State or city in which the alien lives.
与其他规则一致,DHS使用劳工统计局(BLS)全国职业就业和工资估计数据中所有职业的第10百分位小时工资,作为可能获得入门级工资的个人的有效最低工资的合理替代指标。BLS的估计数据考虑了美国劳动力市场工资的变化,该数据每年更新,因此将反映各州最低工资标准的任何变化。目前所有职业的第10百分位小时工资估计值为14.42美元,未计入员工福利。[160]
Consistent with other rules, DHS uses the 10th percentile hourly wage from the Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) National Occupational Employment and Wage Estimates for all occupations as a reasonable proxy for the effective minimum wage for individuals who are likely to earn an entry-level wage. BLS estimates account for changes in wages across the United States labor market, which is updated annually and will thus reflect any changes to State minimum wage rates. The 10th percentile hourly wage estimate for all occupations is currently $14.42, not accounting for worker benefits.[160]
DHS在估算时间机会成本时考虑了员工福利,其方法是使用最新的BLS报告中详述的美国全体平民劳动者平均总薪酬数据,计算出福利与工资的乘数。[161] DHS估计福利与工资的乘数为1.46,该乘数包含员工工资薪金以及带薪休假、保险和退休金等福利的全部成本。[162]因此,运用该福利与工资乘数,DHS计算出总薪酬率为每小时21.05美元,其中第10百分位小时工资估计值为每小时14.42美元,平均福利为每小时6.63美元。[163]
DHS accounts for worker benefits when estimating the opportunity cost of time by calculating a benefits-to-wage multiplier using the most recent BLS report detailing average total employee compensation for all civilian American workers.[161] DHS estimates the benefits-to-wage multiplier to be 1.46, which incorporates employee wages and salaries and the full cost of benefits, such as paid leave, insurance, and retirement.[162] Therefore, using the benefits-to-wage multiplier, DHS calculates the total rate of compensation as $21.05 per hour, where the 10th percentile hourly wage estimate is $14.42 per hour and the average benefits are $6.63 per hour.[163]
利用填写I-912表格所需的时间负担和总薪酬率,DHS估计填写并提交I-912表格的时间机会成本为每份费用减免申请23.05美元。[164]总时间机会成本的计算方法是,将每份费用减免申请的时间机会成本应用于表V.4和表V.6中所示的2019财年至2023财年、2024年4月至2025年3月以及两个时间段之间的中位数批准数量。[165]总时间机会成本的节省将使不再(printed page 37537)具备申请费用减免选项的非军籍外国人受益。
Using the time burden for completing Form I-912 and the total rate of compensation, DHS estimates the opportunity cost of time for completing and submitting Form I-912 would be $23.05 per fee waiver request.[164] The total opportunity cost of time is calculated by applying the opportunity cost of time per fee waiver request to the approvals shown in Table V.4 and Table V.6 for FY 2019 through FY 2023, April 2024 through March 2025, and the median approvals between the two time periods.[165] The total opportunity cost of time would be a cost savings for non- ( printed page 37537) military aliens who would no longer have the option to apply for fee waivers.
表V.11列出了N-400表格和N-336表格费用减免申请的时间机会成本总额(成本节约)。国土安全部(DHS)估计,受取消费用减免影响的非现役人员N-400表格费用减免获批数量将在136,314人(2019财年至2023财年年平均值)至157,488人(2024年4月至2025年3月年度值)之间;[166]两个时期之间的中位数为146,901人。对于不再有资格获得N-400表格费用减免的非现役人员,预计年度成本节约将在3,142,038美元至3,630,098美元之间;预计年度中位成本节约为3,386,068美元(主要估计值)。
Table V.11 shows the total opportunity cost of time (cost savings) for fee waiver requests for Form N-400 and Form N-336. DHS estimates that the number of approved fee waivers for Form N-400 from non-military aliens impacted by the elimination of fee waivers would range between 136,314 (annual average, FY 2019 through FY 2023) and 157,488 (annual, April 2024 through March 2025); [166] the median between these two time periods would be 146,901. The estimated annual cost savings for non-military aliens who would no longer be eligible for fee waivers for Form N-400 would range between $3,142,038 and $3,630,098; the estimated annual median cost savings would be $3,386,068 (primary estimate).
受取消费用减免影响的非现役人员N-336表格费用减免获批数量预计在794人(2019财年至2023财年年平均值)至527人(2024年4月至2025年3月年度值)之间;[167]两个时期之间的中位数为661人。对于不再有资格获得N-336表格费用减免的非现役人员,预计年度成本节约将在18,302美元至12,147美元之间;预计年度中位成本节约为15,236美元(主要估计值)。
The estimated number of approved fee waivers for Form N-336 from non-military aliens impacted by the elimination of fee waivers would range between 794 (annual average, FY 2019 through FY 2023) and 527 (annual, April 2024 through March 2025); [167] the median between these two time periods would be 661. The estimated annual cost savings for non-military aliens who would no longer be eligible for fee waivers for Form N-336 would range between $18,302 and $12,147; the estimated median annual median cost savings would be $15,236 (primary estimate).
国土安全部不预期本拟议规则中所述取消费用减免会对美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的业务运营产生任何成本。国土安全部承认,限制费用减免可能对某些申请人申请入籍福利的能力产生不利影响。
DHS does not anticipate any costs to USCIS operations that would result from the elimination of fee waivers as described in this proposed rule. DHS acknowledges that limiting fee waivers might adversely affect some applicants' ability to apply for naturalization benefits.
国土安全部预期本拟议规则将为美国公民及移民服务局带来定性收益,因为原本用于裁决和处理费用减免申请的资源可以转而用于其他任务或产品线。拟议规则还将降低裁决由无资格入籍或无资格获得费用减免但仍提交申请的外国人所提交的I-912表格、N-400表格和N-336表格的行政成本,这些外国人可能知道自己没有资格,但仍然提交申请,因为如果美国公民及移民服务局最终批准费用减免,申请就有可能是免费的。美国公民及移民服务局也将从获得福利的人员中收回裁决和处理N-400表格及N-336表格的成本。然而,取消费用减免可能导致某些有资格成为归化美国公民的外国人推迟申请并推迟缴纳提高后的费用。这可能导致未来出现更多合法永久居民(LPR)更新其永久居民卡(“绿卡”)的请求,这将对申请人和美国公民及移民服务局造成额外负担。
DHS anticipates this proposed rule would produce a qualitative benefit for USCIS by allowing resources that would have been expended on adjudicating and processing fee waiver requests to instead be devoted to other tasks or product lines. The proposed rule would also reduce administrative costs to adjudicate Forms I-912, N-400 and N-336 that are submitted by aliens who are ineligible for naturalization or a fee waiver, and may know they are not eligible, but they file the application anyway because it would potentially be free if USCIS ultimately approved the fee waiver. USCIS also would recover the cost of adjudicating and processing Form N-400 and Form N-336 from those who receive the benefit. However, eliminating fee waivers might cause certain aliens who are eligible to become naturalized U.S. citizens to defer applying and paying the increased fee. This could result in additional lawful permanent resident (LPR) requests in the future for renewal of their Permanent Resident Cards (“Green” Card) which would be an added burden to applicants and USCIS.
b. 减免费用
b. Reduced Fees
国土安全部提议修订《联邦法规汇编》第8篇第106.2(b)(3)条,终止为申请N-400表格的外国人提供减免费用的资格。目前,如果申请人有文件证明其家庭收入等于或低于联邦贫困指南(FPG)的400%,则该申请人有资格获得减免费用。国土安全部提议要求提交N-400表格的外国人支付全额费用。
DHS proposes to amend 8 CFR 106.2(b)(3) to end the availability of reduced fees for aliens applying for Form N-400. Currently an alien is eligible for a reduced fee if the applicant has a documented household income that is less than or equal to 400 percent of the FPG. DHS proposes to require aliens submitting Form N-400 to pay the full fee.
受拟议取消减免费用影响的人群包括将使用N-400表格申请减免费用的外国人。[168]国土安全部使用已获批的减免费用申请数据(表V.4)来估算拟议取消减免费用对受影响人群的影响。[169]经济影响是按时间段(2019财年至2023财年和2024年4月至2025年3月)估算人群数量,然后使用两个时期人群数量之间的中位数来计算主要估计值。[170]
The populations impacted by the proposed elimination of reduced fees include aliens who would request reduced fees using Form N-400.[168] DHS uses approved requests for reduced fees (Table V.4) to estimate the impact on populations affected by the proposed elimination of reduced fees.[169] Economic impacts are estimated using populations by time period, FY 2019 through FY 2023 and April 2024 through March 2025, then a median between the populations of two periods is used to calculate a primary estimate.[170]
表V.12列出了取消N-400表格减免费用可能产生的转移支付。国土安全部估计,受取消减免费用影响的非现役人员N-400表格减免费用申请获批数量将在2,878人(2019财年至2023财年年平均值)至32,344人(2024年4月至2025年3月年度值)之间;[171]两个时期之间的中位数为17,611人。目前符合非现役减免费用资格的外国人向美国公民及移民服务局缴纳N-400表格年度转移支付预计将在2,734,100美元至30,726,800美元之间;预计年度中位转移支付为16,730,450美元(主要估计值)。
Table V.12 shows the transfers that could result from the elimination of reduced fees for Form N-400. DHS estimates that the number of approved reduced fee requests for Form N-400 from non-military aliens who would be impacted by the elimination of reduced fees would range between 2,878 (annual average, FY 2019 through FY 2023) and 32,344 (annual, April 2024 through March 2025); [171] the median between these two time periods would be 17,611. The estimated annual transfer payments from current non-military reduced fee eligible aliens to USCIS for Form N-400 would range between $2,734,100 and $30,726,800; the estimated median annual transfer payment would be $16,730,450 (primary estimate).
(印刷页第37538页)
( printed page 37538)
申请减费的外国人须填写表格N-400第10部分。美国国土安全部(DHS)估计,目前填写表格N-400第10部分所需的时间负担为0.25小时。[172]由于美国国土安全部提议取消表格N-400的减费资格,第10部分将从表格中删除。因此,申请人将不再需要花费时间填写该部分。时间负担的减少对于将不再能够申请减费的外国人而言是一项成本节省。
Aliens requesting a reduced fee must complete Part 10 of Form N-400. DHS estimates the current time burden for completing Part 10 of Form N-400 is 0.25 hours.[172] Because DHS proposes to eliminate eligibility for reduced fees for Form N-400, Part 10 would be removed from the form. As a result, applicants would no longer spend time completing this section. The decrease in time burden would be a cost savings for aliens who could no longer request a reduced fee.
由于获批减费的外国人已证明其收入与联邦贫困指南(FPG)相比符合资格,我们预计其中很少有人会获得“高档”工资,但我们预计这一群体的工资会高于申请费用减免群体的工资。因此,美国国土安全部使用美国劳工统计局(BLS)全国职业就业和工资估计数据中所有职业的第25百分位小时工资,作为该群体有效最低工资的合理替代指标。目前所有职业的第25百分位小时工资估计为17.66美元,未计入员工福利。[173]
Because aliens approved for reduced fees would have demonstrated they qualified based on their income as compared to the FPG, we would not expect many of them to earn “high-tier” wages, however we would expect this population to earn higher wages than the population requesting fee waivers. Therefore, DHS uses the 25th percentile hourly wage from the BLS National Occupational Employment and Wage Estimates for all occupations as a reasonable proxy for the effective minimum wage for individuals in this population. The 25th percentile hourly wage estimate for all occupations is currently $17.66, not accounting for worker benefits.[173]
美国国土安全部再次通过使用美国劳工统计局最新报告中有关所有美国平民工人平均总薪酬的数据,计算福利与工资的倍数,以估算时间机会成本中的员工福利。[174]美国国土安全部估计福利与工资的倍数为1.46,该倍数包含了员工工资和薪金以及福利的全部成本,如带薪休假、保险和退休金。[175]因此,使用该福利与工资倍数,美国国土安全部计算出总薪酬率为每小时25.78美元,其中第25百分位小时工资估计为每小时17.66美元,平均福利为每小时8.12美元。[176]
Again, DHS accounts for worker benefits when estimating the opportunity cost of time by calculating a benefits-to-wage multiplier using the most recent BLS report detailing average total employee compensation for all civilian American workers.[174] DHS estimates the benefits-to-wage multiplier to be 1.46, which incorporates employee wages and salaries and the full cost of benefits, such as paid leave, insurance, and retirement.[175] Therefore, using the benefits-to-wage multiplier, DHS calculates the total rate of compensation as $25.78 per hour, where the 25th percentile hourly wage estimate is $17.66 per hour and the average benefits are $8.12 per hour.[176]
利用填写表格N-400第10部分的估计时间负担和总薪酬率,美国国土安全部估计,每份减费申请在填写和提交表格N-400第10部分方面的时间机会成本为6.45美元。[177]总时间机会成本的计算方法是,将每份减费申请的时间机会成本应用于表V.4中所示的2019财年至2023财年、2024年4月至2025年3月,以及这两个时期之间的中位数批准数量。[178]对于将不再有资格申请减费的非现役军人外国人而言,总时间机会成本将是一项成本节省。
Using the estimated time burden for completing Part 10 of Form N-400 and the total rate of compensation, DHS estimates the opportunity cost of time for completing and submitting Part 10 of Form N-400 would be $6.45 per fee reduction request.[177] The total opportunity cost of time is calculated by applying the opportunity cost of time per fee reduction request to the approvals shown in Table V.4 for FY 2019 through FY 2023, April 2024 through March 2025, and the median approvals between the two time periods.[178] The total opportunity cost of time would be a cost savings for non-military aliens who would no longer be eligible for reduced fees.
表V.13显示了表格N-400减费申请的总时间机会成本(成本节省)。美国国土安全部估计,受取消减费影响的非现役军人外国人中,表格N-400获批减费申请的数量将在2,878份(2019财年至2023财年年平均数)至32,344份(2024年4月至2025年3月年度数)之间;[179]这两个时期之间的中位数为17,611份。对于将不再能够为表格N-400申请减费的非现役军人外国人而言,估计的年度成本节省将在18,563美元至208,619美元之间;估计的年度成本节省中位数为113,591美元(主要估计值)。
Table V.13 shows the total opportunity cost of time (cost savings) for reduced fee requests for Form N-400. DHS estimates that the number of approved reduced fee requests for Form N-400 from non-military aliens who would be impacted by the elimination of reduced fees would range between 2,878 (annual average, FY 2019 through FY 2023) and 32,344 (annual, April 2024 through March 2025); [179] the median between these two time periods would be 17,611. The estimated annual cost savings for non-military aliens who would no longer be able to apply for reduced fees for Form N-400 would range between $18,563 and $208,619; the estimated median annual cost savings would be $113,591 (primary estimate).
(印刷页第37539页)
( printed page 37539)
美国国土安全部提议为表格N-400的审理和处理收取费用,以便从这些表格中收回提供入籍服务的全部成本。美国国土安全部预计,本提议规则中所述取消减费不会对美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的运营产生任何额外成本。美国国土安全部承认,取消减费可能会对部分申请人申请入籍福利的能力产生不利影响。
DHS is proposing fees for the adjudication and processing of Form N-400 in order to recover full costs of providing naturalization services from those forms. DHS does not anticipate any additional costs to USCIS operations that would result from the elimination of reduced fees as described in this proposed rule. DHS acknowledges that eliminating reduced fees might adversely affect some applicants' ability to apply for naturalization benefits.
美国国土安全部预计,本规则将为美国公民及移民服务局带来定性效益,因为原本用于审理和处理减费申请的资源可以转而用于其他任务或产品线。此外,美国公民及移民服务局将能够收回审理和处理表格N-400的全部成本。然而,取消减费可能导致部分有资格成为归化美国公民的外国人推迟申请并推迟缴纳上调后的费用。这可能导致未来出现更多合法永久居民(LPR)申请更新其永久居民卡(“绿卡”)的情况,这将给申请人和美国公民及移民服务局带来额外负担。
DHS anticipates this rule would produce a qualitative benefit for USCIS by allowing resources that would have been expended on adjudicating and processing reduced fee requests to instead be devoted to other tasks or product lines. Additionally, USCIS would recover the full cost of adjudicating and processing Form N-400. However, eliminating reduced fees might cause certain aliens who are eligible to become naturalized U.S. citizens to defer applying and paying the increased fee. This could result in additional lawful permanent resident (LPR) requests in the future for renewal of their Permanent Resident Cards (“Green” Card) which would be an added burden to applicants and USCIS.

7. 价格弹性
7. Price Elasticity
2024年费用规则提供了关于1989财年至2022财年期间N-400表格价格反应的详细分析。该分析表明，N-400表格费用的历次上调并未导致随时间推移N-400表格申请总数的减少，并且在费用前十次上调中，整体需求并未因此下降。该分析还表明，即使在费用没有变化的年份，N-400表格申请数量也发生了显著变化。相应地，2024年费用规则将N-400表格，或其所授予的（印刷页第37543页）入籍福利，描述为一种缺乏弹性的商品。
The 2024 Fee Rule provided a detailed analysis of the price response to Form N-400 from FY 1989 through FY 2022. The analysis illustrates that past increases in the Form N-400 fee have not led to aggregate reductions in the number of Form N-400 applications filed over time and that overall demand did not decrease in response to the previous ten increases in the Form N-400 fee. It also showed that significant changes in the number of Form N-400 applications filed occurred even in years with no changes to the fee. Correspondingly, the 2024 Fee Rule described the Form N-400, or the ( printed page 37543) citizenship benefit that it confers, as an inelastic good.
此外，N-400表格的申请及相关费用对于寻求入籍的外国人而言是一个不可自由选择的步骤，这意味着符合条件的外国人申请入籍时没有替代表格可用。费用由美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）设定。该费用并非由可因替代品的存在或缺乏而加剧的公开市场供需力量所决定。如果外国人愿意且有资格寻求美国公民身份，则没有其他替代程序或更便宜的选择，因此，如果他们寻求入籍，无论N-400表格费用如何上调，他们最终都要支付一次性申请费。
Further, the application and associated fee for Form N-400 is a non-discretionary step for aliens seeking citizenship, meaning there is no substitute form for eligible aliens to apply for naturalization. USCIS sets the fee for Form N-400. The fee is not set by open-market forces of supply and demand that can be intensified by the availability or lack of substitutes. If aliens are willing and eligible to seek U.S. citizenship, there is no other alternative process or cheaper option, so if they pursue citizenship they ultimately pay the one-time filing fee regardless of any Form N-400 fee increases.
美国国土安全部（DHS）承认，对于某些外国人而言，N-400表格费用可能构成经济负担，表V.4所示的已批准费用豁免和费用减免的数量即可证明这一点。然而，这些外国人必须完成费用豁免申请，无论是使用I-912表格还是提交单独的费用豁免申请，或者在费用减免的情况下，填写N-400表格的单独部分，并提交证明其符合豁免或减免费用资格的所需证据。这一要求表明，费用豁免和费用减免并非任何想少付费的人都可随意获得，而仅限于那些能够证明自己符合当前豁免或减免费用资格标准的人。因此，对费用豁免和费用减免的需求独立于对更低费用的一般需求而存在，其驱动因素是基于经济限制的资格认定，而非更便宜选择的存在。费用减免的需求来自那些自认为符合减免条件的人，而非不符合条件的人。
DHS acknowledges that for some aliens the Form N-400 fee might be a financial burden as may be evidenced by the number of approved fee waivers and reduced fees shown in Table V.4. However, these aliens had to complete a fee waiver request, either by using Form I-912 or submitting a separate fee waiver request, or in the case of the fee reduction completing a separate part of Form N-400, and submit required evidence of eligibility for the waived or reduced fee. This requirement demonstrates that fee waivers and reduced fees are not simply available to anyone who wants to pay less, but only to those who can prove they meet the current eligibility criteria for the waived or reduced fee. Therefore, demand for fee waivers and reduced fees exists independently of general demand for lower fees; it is driven by eligibility based on financial limitations, not the existence of a cheaper option. Demand for fee relief comes from those who believe they qualify for relief, not those who do not qualify.
2024年费用规则保留了N-400表格费用豁免的可获得性，并未改变获得资格的标准。[180] 表V.4显示，2024年4月至2025年3月期间已批准的费用豁免（157,488件）比2019财年至2023财年的五年平均数（136,314件）增加了16%。[181] 在2024年4月至2025年3月期间，有更多申请入籍的外国人符合费用豁免标准，这导致N-400表格收件量增加。2024年费用规则还将N-400表格可获得的费用减免扩大至家庭收入等于或低于联邦贫困指南（FPG）400%的任何外国人。[182] 表V.4显示，2024年4月至2025年3月期间已批准的费用减免（32,344件）比2019财年至2023财年的五年平均数（2,878件）增加了1,024%。[183] 在2024年4月至2025年3月期间，有更多外国人符合费用减免标准，因此，扩大的费用减免资格标准可能是N-400表格收件量增加的催化因素。这些外国人之所以申请费用豁免和费用减免，是因为他们预期由于经济状况而获得批准，而这是更便宜的选择。
The 2024 Fee Rule maintained the availability of fee waivers for Form N-400 and did not change the criteria for being eligible.[180] Table V.4 shows a 16 percent increase in approved fee waivers for April 2024 through March 2025 (157,488) from the 5-year average of FY 2019 through FY 2023 (136,314).[181] More aliens who applied for naturalization met the criteria for fee waivers during April 2024 through March 2025 which resulted in increases in Form N-400 receipts. The 2024 Fee Rule also expanded the reduced fee available for Form N-400 to any alien who has a household income at or under 400 percent of the FPG.[182] Table V.4 shows there was a 1,024 percent increase in approved reduced fees for April 2024 through March 2025 (32,344) from the 5-year average of FY 2019 through FY 2023 (2,878).[183] More aliens met the criteria for reduced fees during April 2024 through March 2025, thus the expanded eligibility criteria for the reduced fees likely acted as the catalyst for increased N-400 receipts. These aliens applied for fee waivers and reduced fees because of the expectation of being approved due to financial circumstances, which was the cheaper option.
美国国土安全部(DHS)审查并考虑了Hainmueller、Lawrence、Gest、Hotard、Koslowski和Laitin(2018年)以及Yasenov、Hotard、Lawrence和Laitin(2019年)关于低收入移民入籍率的研究,因为这些论文也在2024年费用规则中得到审查。[184] [185] Hainmueller等人建议,“为了降低所有低收入移民的费用,同时覆盖行政服务的全部成本,美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)可以引入多层次费用结构,让较富裕的申请人支付更高的费用”,以降低入籍的财务障碍。Yasenov等人发现,“高昂的申请费用和获得费用减免的困难是低收入合法永久居民(LPR)获得公民身份的障碍。”如本序言所述,在2024年费用规则实施后的日历年内,更多外国人符合费用减免资格标准,但费用($0)或资格标准均未发生变化。DHS在本拟议规则中重申,如2024年费用规则所述,对降低费用或将费用维持在$0所观察到的增长,不能直接推断出费用上涨时的价格弹性或收入弹性。对费用减免的需求独立于对全价I-400表格的需求而存在。对费用减免的需求来自那些认为自己符合减免资格的人,而非不符合资格的人。基于这些原因以及本序言前文所述的其他原因,DHS维持其立场,即I-400表格或其所赋予的公民身份权益属于缺乏弹性的商品。
DHS reviewed and considered research on naturalization rates of low-income immigrants by Hainmueller, Lawrence, Gest, Hotard, Koslowski, and Laitin (2018) as well as Yasenov, Hotard, Lawrence, and Laitin (2019), as these papers were also reviewed in the 2024 Fee Rule.[184] [185] Hainmueller et al. suggest “[t]o reduce fees for all low-income immigrants while covering the full cost of administrative services, USCIS could introduce a multitiered fee structure in which wealthier applicants pay higher fees” to lower the financial barrier to naturalization. Yasenov et al. find that “high application fees and difficulties accessing the fee waiver are barriers to citizenship for low-income LPRs.” As described in this preamble, more aliens met the criteria for fee waivers during the calendar year after the 2024 Fee Rule but there were no changes in the fee ($0) or change in eligibility criteria. DHS reiterates in this proposed rule, as was stated in the 2024 Fee Rule, an observed increase in response to lowering or keeping fees at $0 cannot be directly extrapolated to yield price or income elasticity for an increase in fees. Demand for fee waivers exists independently from demand for Form N-400 at full price. Demand for fee relief comes from those who believe they qualify for relief, not those who do not qualify. For these reasons and the others described earlier in this preamble, DHS maintains that Form N-400, or the citizenship benefit that it confers, is an inelastic good.
本规则中的拟议变更将仅影响I-400表格和I-336表格,USCIS使用基于活动的成本法(ABC方法)来评估当前费用是否收回每种表格裁决的全部相应成本。本序言前文提及的两项研究表明,应更多采用支付能力原则,以促进低收入移民的入籍。然而,本拟议规则的目的并非要求某些较高收入的移民群体承担更高的费用,以补贴较低收入群体的入籍费用。本规则建议将DHS针对这些表格的费用设定重点重新调整为受益人付费原则。如序言所述,受益人付费原则有助于促进经济效率。因此,费用设定过低可能导致机构资源和服务被过度使用。
The proposed changes in this rule would impact only Form N-400 and Form N-336, and USCIS uses the ABC methodology to evaluate whether current fees recover the full proportional costs of adjudicating each form. The two studies mentioned earlier in this preamble suggest more of an ability-to-pay principle to promote citizenship for low-income immigrants. However, it is not the intent of this proposed rule to have certain higher-income immigrant populations endure higher fees to subsidize naturalization for lower-income populations. This rule proposes to return the focus of DHS fee-setting for these forms to the beneficiary-pays principle. As described in the preamble, the beneficiary-pays principle promotes economic efficiency; therefore, setting a fee too low can induce overuse of agency resources and services.
8. 替代方案
8. Alternatives
DHS考虑了维持I-400表格现行费用和I-336表格现行费用的可能性。然而,维持I-400表格现行普通费用(纸质申请$760,在线申请$710)所导致的年度成本/收入差距估计为$8,240万(纸质申请)和$2.322亿(在线申请)。维持I-336表格现行普通费用(纸质申请$830,在线申请$780)所导致的年度成本/收入差距估计为$60万(纸质申请)和$110万(在线申请)。[186]
DHS considered the possibility of maintaining the current fee for Form N-400 and the current fee for Form N-336. However, the annual cost/revenue gap for keeping the current general fees for Form N-400 of $760 (paper) and $710 (online) is estimated at $82.4 million (paper) and $232.2 million (online). The annual cost/revenue gap for keeping the current general fees for Form N-336 of $830 (paper) and $780 (online) is estimated at $0.6 million (paper) and $1.1 million (online).[186]
DHS可以允许非军人外国申请人继续有资格申请I-400表格和I-336表格的费用减免。然而,DHS指出,与现行普通费用相比,I-400表格因费用减免导致的估计年度成本/收入差距为$1.092亿,I-336表格为$50万(表III.3)。与2024年费用规则ABC模型输出结果相比,因费用减免导致的估计成本/收入差距,I-400表格为$1.666亿,I-336表格为$74万。
DHS could allow non-military alien applicants to remain eligible for fee waivers for Form N-400 and Form N-336. However, DHS notes that the estimated annual cost/revenue gap from fee waivers versus the current general fee for Form N-400 is $109.2 million and for Form N-336 is $0.5 million (Table III.3). The estimated cost/revenue gap from fee waivers versus the 2024 Fee Rule ABC model output for Form N-400 is $166.6 million and for Form N-336 is $0.74 million.
DHS还可以允许非军人外国申请人继续有资格就I-400表格申请减费。与现行普通费用相比,因减费导致的估计年度成本/收入差距,纸质申请为$430万,在线申请为$590万(表III.2)。与2024年费用规则ABC模型输出结果相比,因减费导致的估计成本/收入差距(第37544页),I-400表格纸质申请为$850万,在线申请为$1,280万。
DHS could also allow non-military alien applicants to remain eligible for a reduced fee for Form N-400. The estimated annual cost/revenue gap from reduced fees versus the current general fee for Form N-400 is $4.3 million from paper filings and $5.9 million for online filings (Table III.2). The estimated cost/revenue gap from reduced fees versus the 2024 Fee Rule ABC model output for ( printed page 37544) Form N-400 is $8.5 million for paper filings and $12.8 million for online filings.
DHS决定不采用维持现行费用结构的替代方案。根据受益人付费的费用设定方法,拟议费用将收回其裁决的全部成本。这些拟议变更旨在使I-400表格和I-336表格的费用与处理这些表格的成本保持一致,同时维持法定合规性并支持USCIS的财务可持续性。
DHS decided against the alternative of maintaining the current fee structure. The proposed fees would recover the full cost of their adjudication, according to the beneficiary-pays approach to fee setting. These proposed changes aim to align the fees for Form N-400 and Form N-336 with the costs of processing those forms, while maintaining statutory compliance and supporting USCIS' financial sustainability.
B. 《监管灵活性法案》
B. Regulatory Flexibility Act
1980年《监管灵活性法案》(5 U.S.C. 601-612),经1996年《小企业监管执行公平法案》(SBREFA)(1996年3月29日第104-121号公共法)修订,要求联邦机构在制定规章时考虑法规对小企业、小型政府辖区和小型组织的潜在影响。“小实体”一词包括小企业、独立拥有和经营且在其所属领域不占主导地位的非营利组织,或人口少于50,000人的政府辖区。[187]美国国土安全部(DHS)拟调整表格N-400和表格N-336的费用,并取消这两种表格的费用减免,以及取消外国人和美国国民申请表格N-400时适用的减收费用。受本规则影响的人群包括提交表格N-400或表格N-336或申请费用减免的外国人。
The Regulatory Flexibility Act of 1980, 5 U.S.C. 601-612, as amended by the Small Business Regulatory Enforcement Fairness Act of 1996 (SBREFA), Public Law 104-121 (Mar. 29, 1996), requires Federal agencies to consider the potential impact of regulations on small businesses, small governmental jurisdictions, and small organizations during the development of their rules. The term “small entities” comprises small businesses, not-for-profit organizations that are independently owned and operated and are not dominant in their fields, or governmental jurisdictions with populations of less than 50,000.[187] DHS proposes to adjust the fees for Form N-400 and Form N-336 and eliminate fee waivers for both forms and the reduced fee for aliens and U.S. nationals filing Form N-400. The populations impacted by this rule would include aliens who submit Form N-400 or Form N-336 or request a fee waiver.
本拟议规则不直接规范小实体,预计不会对小实体产生直接影响。相反,本拟议规则直接规范并影响申请入籍福利的外国人,而根据《监管灵活性法案》,个人不被视为“小实体”。根据本分析及本序言通篇所呈现的证据,DHS证明本拟议规则不会对大量小实体造成重大经济影响。
This proposed rule does not directly regulate small entities and is not expected to have a direct effect on small entities. Rather, this proposed rule directly regulates and impacts aliens requesting naturalization benefits, and individuals are not considered “small entities” under the Regulatory Flexibility Act. Based on the evidence presented in this analysis and throughout this preamble, DHS certifies that this proposed rule would not have a significant economic impact on a substantial number of small entities.
C. 1995年《无资金授权改革法案》
C. Unfunded Mandates Reform Act of 1995
1995年《无资金授权改革法案》(UMRA)旨在遏制向州、地方和部落政府施加无资金联邦授权的做法,以及其他目的。[188] UMRA第二编要求每个联邦机构就拟议规则或已就其发布拟议规则的最终规则中任何联邦授权的影响编写书面声明,其中包括可能导致州、地方和部落政府合计或私营部门在任何一年内支出1亿美元或以上(每年根据通货膨胀进行调整)的任何联邦授权。见2 U.S.C. 1532(a)。根据面向所有城市消费者的消费者价格指数(CPI-U),1995年1亿美元的通胀调整价值在2025年约为2.13亿美元。[189]
The Unfunded Mandates Reform Act of 1995 (UMRA) is intended, among other things, to curb the practice of imposing unfunded Federal mandates on State, local, and Tribal governments.[188] Title II of UMRA requires each Federal agency to prepare a written statement assessing the effects of any Federal mandate in a proposed rule, or final rule for which the agency published a proposed rule, which includes any Federal mandate that may result in a $100 million or more expenditure (adjusted annually for inflation) in any one year by State, local, and Tribal governments, in the aggregate, or by the private sector. See2 U.S.C. 1532(a). The inflation adjusted value of $100 million in 1995 is approximately $213 million in 2025 based on the Consumer Price Index for All Urban Consumers (CPI-U).[189]
本拟议规则不包含此类授权,因为它不会对任何其他级别的政府或私营部门实体施加任何可强制执行的义务。相反,由于该规则将把基础设施项目的预留份额法典化,可能会产生一些公私合作投资项目,并为州或地方政府带来有益的下游影响。任何此类实体的下游影响都仅因其自愿选择以及他人的自愿选择而产生,并非本规则所施加的可强制执行义务的后果。同样,州和地方政府产生的任何成本或转移效应,也并非源于UMRA所定义的联邦授权。因此,UMRA第二编的要求不适用,DHS未根据UMRA编写声明。但是,DHS已在本序言第V.A节的监管影响分析(RIA)中分析了本拟议行动的许多潜在影响。
This proposed rule does not contain such a mandate, because it would not impose any enforceable duty upon any other level of government or private sector entity. Rather, there might be some private-public partnership investment projects and beneficial downstream effects to State or local governments because the rule would codify the set aside for infrastructure projects. Any downstream effects on such entities would arise solely due to their voluntary choices, and the voluntary choices of others, and would not be a consequence of an enforceable duty imposed by this rule. Similarly, any costs or transfer effects on State and local governments would not result from a Federal mandate as that term is defined under UMRA. The requirements of title II of UMRA, therefore, do not apply, and DHS has not prepared a statement under UMRA. DHS has, however, analyzed many of the potential effects of this proposed action in the RIA in Section V.A of this preamble.
D. 第13132号行政命令(联邦制)
D. Executive Order 13132 (Federalism)
本拟议规则不会对各州、国家政府与各州之间的关系,或各级政府之间权力和职责的分配产生实质性直接影响。因此,根据第13132号行政命令《联邦制》第6节,确定本拟议规则不具有足够的联邦制影响,无需编写联邦制摘要影响声明。
This proposed rule would not have substantial direct effects on the States, on the relationship between the National Government and the States, or on the distribution of power and responsibilities among the various levels of government. Therefore, in accordance with section 6 of E.O. 13132, Federalism, it is determined that this proposed rule does not have sufficient federalism implications to warrant the preparation of a federalism summary impact statement.
E. 第12988号行政命令(民事司法改革)
E. Executive Order 12988 (Civil Justice Reform)
本拟议规则根据第12988号行政命令《民事司法改革》起草和审查。本拟议规则旨在为受影响行为提供明确的法律标准,并经过仔细审查以消除起草错误和歧义,从而尽量减少诉讼和对联邦法院系统造成的不当负担。DHS已确定本拟议规则符合第12988号行政命令第3节规定的适用标准。
This proposed rule is drafted and reviewed in accordance with E.O. 12988, Civil Justice Reform. This proposed rule was written to provide a clear legal standard for affected conduct and was reviewed carefully to eliminate drafting errors and ambiguities, so as to minimize litigation and undue burden on the Federal Court system. DHS has determined that this proposed rule meets the applicable standards provided in section 3 of E.O. 12988.
F. 家庭评估
F. Family Assessment
DHS已依照1999年《财政和一般拨款法》第654条[190]的要求审查了本规则,该法作为1999年《综合合并及紧急补充拨款法》的一部分颁布。[191] DHS通过评估本监管行动是否:(1)影响家庭的稳定性或安全性,特别是在婚姻承诺方面;(2)影响父母在子女教育、养育和监管方面的权力;(3)有助于家庭履行其职能或以政府行为取代该职能;(4)影响家庭和儿童的可支配收入或贫困状况;(5)仅在有正当理由的范围内对家庭产生财务影响(如果有的话);(6)可以由州或地方政府或由家庭自行实施;或(7)就青少年行为与个人责任之间的关系以及社会规范确立政策,系统地审查了《财政和一般拨款法》第654(c)条所规定的标准。如果本机构认定某项法规可能对家庭福祉产生负面影响,则本机构必须为其实施提供充分的理由。
DHS has reviewed this rule in line with the requirements of section 654 of the Treasury and General Appropriations Act, 1999,[190] enacted as part of the Omnibus Consolidated and Emergency Supplemental Appropriations Act, 1999.[191] DHS has systematically reviewed the criteria specified in section 654(c) of the Treasury and General Appropriations Act by evaluating whether this regulatory action: (1) impacts the stability or safety of the family, particularly in terms of marital commitment; (2) impacts the authority of parents in the education, nurture, and supervision of their children; (3) helps the family perform its functions or substitutes governmental activity for the function; (4) affects disposable income or poverty of families and children; (5) only financially impacts families, if at all, to the extent such impacts are justified; (6) may be carried out by State or local government or by the family; or (7) establishes a policy concerning the relationship between the behavior and personal responsibility of youth and the norms of society. If the agency determines a regulation may negatively affect family well-being, then the agency must provide an adequate rationale for its implementation.
DHS拟调整美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)就I-400表格和N-336表格收取的费用,取消N-400表格的减免费选项,并取消这两种表格的费用豁免。通过提高N-400表格和N-336表格的费用,本项(印刷页第37545页)监管行动可能对某些家庭成员拟申请入籍或因家庭成员入籍申请被拒而申请听证的家庭造成更高的经济负担。因此,本规则可能影响拟申请入籍的外国人的部分家庭和子女的可支配收入,或要求这些外国人推迟入籍。这两种情形都可能对外国人的家庭造成经济影响。然而,DHS认为,这些潜在的不利影响可因使USCIS表格费用与审理这些表格的成本保持一致这一目标而具有正当性,该目标符合授权收取费用的相关法规。费用上调将仅限于N-400表格和N-336表格,并将符合《移民与国籍法》(INA)第286(m)条和第344条,即8 U.S.C. 1356(m)和1455,以及31 U.S.C. 9701的规定。DHS将保留对现役军人的费用豁免以及对在线申请人的50美元折扣。DHS认为,本拟议规则一旦最终确定,所提高费用带来的收益将为对部分家庭造成的经济影响提供充分的正当理由。DHS还确定,本规则制定不会以其他方式影响家庭作为一种制度的自主性或完整性。
DHS proposes to adjust the fees that USCIS charges for Form N-400 and Form N-336, eliminate the reduced fee option for Form N-400, and eliminate the availability of fee waivers for both forms. By increasing the Form N-400 and the Form N-336 fees, this ( printed page 37545) regulatory action may impose a higher financial burden on some families whose members may seek to naturalize or request a hearing due to the denial of a family member's naturalization application. Therefore, this rule could potentially affect disposable income of some families and children of aliens intending to naturalize or require those aliens to defer naturalization. Both scenarios could impact aliens' families financially. However, DHS believes that those potential adverse impacts would be justified by the goal of aligning fees for USCIS forms with costs of adjudicating those forms in line with the statutes that authorize the fees. The fee increases would be limited to Form N-400 and Form N-336 and would comply with sections 286(m) and 344 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1356(m) and 1455, as well as 31 U.S.C. 9701. DHS would retain fee exemptions for service members of the armed forces and the $50 discount for online filers. DHS believes that the benefits of the increased fees in this proposed rule, once finalized, would provide sufficient justification for the financial impact on some families. DHS has also determined that this rulemaking would not otherwise have an impact on the autonomy or integrity of the family as an institution.
G. 第13175号行政命令(与印第安部落政府的协商与协调)
G. Executive Order 13175 (Consultation and Coordination With Indian Tribal Governments)
根据关于与印第安部落政府协商与协调的第13175号行政命令,本拟议规则不涉及部落事项,因为它不会对一个或多个印第安部落、联邦政府与印第安部落之间的关系,或联邦政府与印第安部落之间权力和责任的分配产生实质性直接影响。
This proposed rule would not have Tribal implications under Executive Order 13175, Consultation and Coordination with Indian Tribal Governments, because it would not have a substantial direct effect on one or more Indian Tribes, on the relationship between the Federal Government and Indian Tribes, or on the distribution of power and responsibilities between the Federal Government and Indian Tribes.
H. 国家环境政策法
H. National Environmental Policy Act
DHS及其下属机构对拟议的监管行动进行分析,以确定《国家环境政策法》(NEPA),42 U.S.C. 4321及其后续条款是否适用,如适用,则确定所需的分析程度。DHS第023-01号指令(修订版01)《国家环境政策法的实施》(第023-01号指令修订版01)以及第023-01-001-01号指导手册(修订版01)(《指导手册》)[192]规定了DHS及其下属机构用以遵守NEPA的政策和程序。
DHS and its components analyze proposed regulatory actions to determine whether the National Environmental Policy Act (NEPA), 42 U.S.C. 4321, et seq., applies and, if so, what degree of analysis is required. DHS Directive 023-01 Rev. 01 “Implementing the National Environmental Policy Act” (Dir. 023- 01 Rev. 01) and Instruction Manual 023-01-001-01 Rev. 01 (Instruction Manual) [192] establish the policies and procedures that DHS and its components use to comply with NEPA.
NEPA允许联邦机构在其NEPA实施程序中设立若干行动类别(“分类排除”),根据经验,这些行动单独或累积均不会对人类环境产生重大影响,因此不需要进行环境评估或环境影响声明。[193]《指导手册》附录A列出了DHS的分类排除事项。[194]
NEPA allows Federal agencies to establish, in their NEPA implementing procedures, categories of actions (“categorical exclusions”) that experience has shown do not, individually or cumulatively, have a significant effect on the human environment and, therefore, do not require an environmental assessment or environmental impact statement.[193] The Instruction Manual, Appendix A lists the DHS Categorical Exclusions.[194]
根据国土安全部(DHS)《国家环境政策法》(NEPA)实施程序的规定,某项行动要获得排除类别认定,必须满足以下三个条件:(1)整个行动明显属于一项或多项排除类别;(2)该行动不是更大规模行动的组成部分;且(3)不存在可能产生重大环境影响的特殊情况。[195]
Under DHS NEPA implementing procedures, for an action to be categorically excluded, it must satisfy each of the following three conditions: (1) The entire action clearly fits within one or more of the categorical exclusions; (2) the action is not a piece of a larger action; and (3) no extraordinary circumstances exist that create the potential for a significant environmental effect.[195]
本拟议规则仅限于调整表格I-400号和I-336号的费用,包括取消这些表格的费用减免和降费待遇,以使其费用能够按照“受益人付费”收费确定方法,完全收回审理成本。这些拟议变更一旦最终确定,旨在使费用与办理I-400号和I-336号表格的成本保持一致,同时确保符合法定要求并支持美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)的财务可持续性。因此,本拟议规则仅对现行国土安全部关于USCIS收费表的法规进行修订。国土安全部已对本拟议规则进行审查,认定本拟议规则所颁布的修正案不会对环境造成重大影响,也不会导致环境影响发生任何变化。
This proposed rule is limited to adjusting the fees for Form N-400 and Form N-336, including eliminating fee waivers and reduced fees for these forms, so that their fees recover the full cost of the adjudication, according to the beneficiary-pays approach to the fee setting. These proposed changes, once finalized, aim to align fees with the costs of processing Form N-400 and Form N-336 while maintaining statutory compliance and supporting USCIS' financial sustainability. As such, this proposed rule is only amending existing DHS regulations governing the USCIS fee schedule. DHS has reviewed this proposed rule and finds that no significant impact on the environment, or any change in environmental effect, will result from the amendments being promulgated in this proposed rule.
据此,国土安全部认定,本拟议规则对现行法规所作的修正案明显属于国土安全部《国家环境政策法》实施程序中确立的排除类别A3,即不会改变环境影响的行政性变更,且不属于更大规模联邦行动的组成部分,也不存在可能产生重大环境影响的特殊情况。因此,本拟议监管修正案应予以排除类别认定,无需进行进一步的《国家环境政策法》审查。
Accordingly, DHS finds that the promulgation of this proposed rule's amendments to current regulations clearly fits within categorical exclusion A3 established in DHS's NEPA implementing procedures as an administrative change with no change in environmental effect, is not part of a larger Federal action, and does not present extraordinary circumstances that create the potential for a significant environmental effect. Therefore, the proposed regulatory amendments are categorically excluded from further NEPA review.
一、文书削减法
I. Paperwork Reduction Act
根据1995年《文书削减法》(PRA),《美国法典》第44编第3501节至第3512节的规定,国土安全部必须将规则中固有的任何报告要求提交管理和预算办公室(OMB)审查和批准,除非该等要求获得豁免。请参阅随附的《文书削减法》文件以获取完整分析。表V.19“信息收集”列出了属于本规则制定内容的信息收集项目。

Please see the accompanying PRA documentation for the full analysis. Table V.19, Information Collections, lists the information collections that are part of this rulemaking.

1. USCIS 表格I-912号
1. USCIS Form I-912
信息收集概述:
Overview of information collection:
(1)信息收集类型:对现已批准的收集项目进行修订。
(1) Type of Information Collection: Revision of a Currently Approved Collection.
(2)表格/收集项目名称:费用减免申请书。
(2) Title of the Form/Collection: Request for Fee Waiver.
(3)机构表格编号(如有)及国土安全部负责该收集项目的相应部门:I-912号;USCIS。
(3) Agency form number, if any, and the applicable component of the DHS sponsoring the collection: I-912; USCIS.
(4)将被要求或请求作答的受影响公众,以及简要说明:主要对象为个人或家庭。USCIS利用本表格收集的数据,核实申请人是否无力支付所申请移民福利的费用。当申请人或请愿人根据《联邦法规汇编》第8编第106.3条明确证明其符合资格时,USCIS将考虑为其申请书或请愿书减免费用。I-912号表格使申请人就费用减免请求所提供的陈述和佐证文件的收集与分析实现标准化。I-912号表格还通过清晰列明判定是否无力支付所需的最主要数据和证据,简化并加快了USCIS对费用减免请求的批准或驳回程序。官员在作出最终决定时,会评估为支持费用减免请求所提交的全部信息和证据。每个案件都是独一无二的,将根据其自身情况加以审议。如果费用减免获得批准,该申请将予以受理并进行处理。如果费用减免未获批准,USCIS将通知申请人,并指示其按相应费用重新提交申请。
(4) Affected public who will be asked or required to respond, as well as a brief abstract: Primary: Individuals or households. USCIS uses the data collected on this form to verify that the applicant is unable to pay for the immigration benefit being requested. USCIS will consider waiving a fee for an application or petition when the applicant or petitioner clearly demonstrates he or she is eligible based on 8 CFR 106.3. Form I-912 standardizes the collection and analysis of statements and supporting documentation provided by the applicant with the fee waiver request. Form I-912 also streamlines and expedites USCIS' approval or rejection of the fee waiver request by clearly laying out the most salient data and evidence necessary for the determination of inability to pay. Officers evaluate all information and evidence supplied in support of a fee waiver request when making a final determination. Each case is unique and is considered on its own merits. If the fee waiver is granted, the application will be processed. If the fee waiver is not granted, USCIS will notify the applicant and instruct him or her to file a new application with the appropriate fee.
（5）对受访者总数的估计以及平均每位受访者所需答复时间的估计：表格I-912（纸质版）信息收集的预估受访者总数为380,181人，每次答复的预估小时负担为1.095小时；表格I-912（PDFi）信息收集的预估受访者总数为65,742人，每次答复的预估小时负担为1小时；非表格费用减免申请（纸质版）信息收集的预估受访者总数为7,470人，每次答复的预估小时负担为1.095小时；非表格费用减免申请（PDFi）信息收集的预估受访者总数为930人，每次答复的预估小时负担为1小时；根据8 CFR 103.7(d)提出的局长例外请求信息收集的预估受访者总数为128人，每次答复的预估小时负担为1.095小时。

（6）与该信息收集相关的公众总负担（以小时计）的估计：与此信息收集相关的预估年度总小时负担为491,290小时。
(6) An estimate of the total public burden (in hours) associated with the collection: The total estimated annual hour burden associated with this collection is 491,290 hours.

(7) An estimate of the total public burden (in cost) associated with the collection: The estimated total annual cost burden associated with this collection of information is $1,453,691.

美国国土安全部（DHS）和美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）邀请社会公众及其他联邦机构就拟议信息收集对各方的影响发表意见。根据《文书削减法》（PRA），本信息收集通知在《联邦公报》上公布，以征求对拟议信息收集文件修改内容的意见。

信息收集概述:
Overview of information collection:
(1) 信息收集类型:对现已获批准的信息收集的修订。
(1) Type of Information Collection: Revision of a Currently Approved Collection.
(2) 表格/收集名称:入籍申请书。
(2) Title of the Form/Collection: Application for Naturalization.
(3) 机构表格编号(如有)及负责该信息收集的国土安全部(DHS)相关部门: N-400表;美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)。
(3) Agency form number, if any, and the applicable component of DHS sponsoring the collection: N-400; USCIS.
(4) 将被要求或可能被要求作出回应的受影响公众,以及简要摘要:主要为:个人或家庭。N-400表《入籍申请书》使美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)能够履行其公正裁决入籍申请、仅使符合法定资格的个人入籍的使命。入籍是指外国人在满足国会在《移民与国籍法》中规定的各项要求后被授予美国公民身份的程序。
(4) Affected public who will be asked or required to respond, as well as a brief abstract: Primary: Individuals or households. Form N-400, Application for Naturalization, allows USCIS to fulfill its mission of fairly adjudicating naturalization applications and only naturalizing statutorily eligible individuals. Naturalization is the process by which U.S. citizenship is granted to an alien after fulfilling the requirements established by Congress in the Act.
(5) 对受访者总数及平均受访者所需答复时间的估算:N-400表(纸质提交)信息收集的受访者估计总数为454,850人,每份答复的估计小时负担为8.547小时;N-400表(在线提交)信息收集的受访者估计总数为454,850人,每份答复的估计小时负担为3.92小时;生物特征信息提交的受访者估计总数为909,700人,每份答复的估计小时负担为1.17小时。
(5) An estimate of the total number of respondents and the amount of time estimated for an average respondent to respond: The estimated total number of respondents for the information collection Form N-400 (paper filed) is 454,850 and the estimated hour burden per response is 8.547 hours; the estimated total number of respondents for the information collection Form N-400 (online filed) is 454,850 and the estimated hour burden per response is 3.92 hours; the estimated total number of respondents for the information collection biometrics submission is 909,700 and the estimated hour burden per response is 1.17 hours.
(6) 与该信息收集相关的公众总负担(以小时计)的估算:与此次信息收集相关的估计年度总小时负担为6,734,964小时。
(6) An estimate of the total public burden (in hours) associated with the collection: The total estimated annual hour burden associated with this collection is 6,734,964 hours.
(7) 与该信息收集相关的公众总负担(以费用计)的估算:与此信息收集相关的估计年度总费用负担为421,645,950美元。
(7) An estimate of the total public burden (in cost) associated with the collection: The estimated total annual cost burden associated with this collection of information is $421,645,950.

3. 外国人是指非美国公民或美国国民的人。参见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第101(a)(3)条,8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(3)。但是,非公民的美国国民也可根据《移民与国籍法》申请入籍。《移民与国籍法》将美国国民定义为美国公民,或“虽非美国公民但对美国负有永久效忠义务的人”。参见《移民与国籍法》第101(a)(22)条,8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(22)。为便于阅读本前言,本前言将使用“外国人”或“申请人”一词,尽管相关描述同样适用于非美国公民的美国国民。
3. An alien is defined as a person not a citizen or national of the United States. See Immigration and Nationality Act (INA) sec. 101(a)(3), 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(3). However, nationals of the United States who are not citizens may also apply for naturalization under the INA. The INA defines a national of the United States as either a U.S. citizen or “a person who, though not a citizen of the United States, owes permanent allegiance to the United States.” See INA sec. 101(a)(22), 8 U.S.C. 1101(a)(22). For ease of reading this preamble, the preamble will refer to “alien” or “applicant,” although the descriptions equally apply to U.S. nationals who are not U.S. citizens.

7. I-912表,即费用豁免申请表,必须与N-400表或N-336表一同提交。I-912表将在N-400表或N-336表获得裁定之前先行裁定。若I-912表获批准,USCIS将为N-400表或N-336表签发收据通知,并继续裁定N-400表或N-336表。若I-912表被拒绝,整份申请材料将退还申请人。见USCIS,I-912表,《费用豁免申请须知》,https://www.uscis.gov/​sites/​default/​files/​document/​forms/​i-912instr.pdf(2025年7月22日版)。
7. Form I-912, or a fee waiver request, must be submitted together with Form N-400 or with Form N-336. Form I-912 is adjudicated before the Form N-400 or Form N-336 is adjudicated. If the Form I-912 is approved, USCIS will issue a receipt notice for the Form N-400 or Form N-336 and proceed with adjudicating Form N-400 or Form N-336. If the Form I-912 is denied, the entire application package will be returned to the applicant. See USCIS, Form I-912, “Instructions for Request for Fee Waiver,” https://www.uscis.gov/​sites/​default/​files/​document/​forms/​i-912instr.pdf (July 22, 2025 ed.).

8. 自2003年3月1日起,原移民归化局(INS)作为美国司法部(DOJ)下属机构不复存在,其关于移民福利申请及入籍事务的职能已移交至美国国土安全部下属的美国公民及移民服务局。见《国土安全法》第451(b)节和第471节,6 U.S.C. 271(b)、291。尽管本拟议规则中讨论的《移民与国籍法》(INA)若干条款仅提及“司法部长”,但根据《国土安全法》的规定,此类条款现应理解为指国土安全部部长。见例如INA第103(a)(1)节、第286节、第344节,8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(1)、1356、1455;Nielsen诉Preap案,139 S. Ct. 954, 959 n.2 (2019)。
8. As of March 1, 2003, the former Immigration and Naturalization Service (INS) ceased to exist as an agency within the United States Department of Justice (DOJ) and its functions regarding the applications for immigration benefits and naturalizations were transferred to the United States Citizenship and Immigration Services in the United States Department of Homeland Security. See HSA secs. 451(b) and 471, 6 U.S.C. 271(b), 291. Although several provisions of the INA discussed in this proposed rule refer exclusively to the “Attorney General,” such provisions are now to be read as referring to the Secretary of Homeland Security by operation of the HSA. See, e.g., INA secs. 103(a)(1), 286, 344, 8 U.S.C. 1103(a)(1), 1356, 1455; Nielsen v. Preap, 139 S. Ct. 954, 959 n.2 (2019).
返回引文
Back to Citation
9. 国土安全部长期以来的解释是,INA第286(m)节(8 U.S.C. 1356(m))中的“包括”条款并不限制国土安全部根据该法规拥有的收费权限。该“包括”条款仅提供了国土安全部在考虑裁定和入籍服务全部成本时可计入的部分费用的非穷尽清单。见INA第286(m)节,8 U.S.C. 1356(m);《联邦公报》第84卷第23930页,第23932页注1(2019年5月23日);第81卷第26904页,第26906页注10。
9. The longstanding interpretation of DHS is that the “including” clause in INA sec. 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m), does not constrain DHS's fee authority under the statute. The “including” clause offers only a non-exhaustive list of some of the costs that DHS may consider part of the full costs of providing adjudication and naturalization services. See INA sec. 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m); 84 FR 23930, 23932 n.1 (May 23, 2019); 81 FR 26904, 26906 n.10.
返回引文
Back to Citation
10. 与此前的规则制定一致,国土安全部并不将31 U.S.C. 9701条解释为授予其设定USCIS费用的独立权力。见《联邦公报》第89卷第6194页,第6246页(2024年1月31日)。美国大多数收取费用的政府机构,是依据《独立机构拨款法》(IOAA)收费的,该法编入31 U.S.C. 9701(其中规定,除其他事项外,“每项收费应……基于……政府的成本[以及]该服务或物品对接受者的价值”)。然而,国会已指示以费用而非拨款方式为移民福利提供资金。见《1989年度商务部、司法部、国务院、司法机关及相关机构拨款法》,公法第100-459号第209节,102 Stat. 2186(1988年)(编入INA第286(m)节,8 U.S.C. 1356(m))。然而,INA第344(a)节,8 U.S.C. 1455,通过引用方式纳入了31 U.S.C. 9701中的指导原则。1988年10月24日,国会修订了INA第344(a)节,8 U.S.C. 1455,加入了对31 U.S.C. 9701的引用。见公法第100-525号第9(ff)节,102 Stat. 2609(1988年)。1990年,国会在未修改INA第344节(8 U.S.C. 1455)的情况下,修订了INA第286(m)节(8 U.S.C. 1356(m)),指示移民审查费账户(IEFA)为提供“裁定和入籍服务”的全部成本提供资金,包括向移民免费提供的服务,见公法第101-515号第210(d)(1)和(2)节,104 Stat. 2101, 2121(1990年11月5日),从而提供了广泛的法定权限,可综合考量INA第286(m)节和第344节(8 U.S.C. 1356和1455)以及31 U.S.C. 9701中所列举的所有因素。
10. Consistent with prior rulemakings DHS does not interpret 31 U.S.C. 9701 as providing independent authority for DHS to set USCIS fees. See 89 FR 6194, 6246 (Jan. 31, 2024). Most U.S. government agencies that charge fees do so under the Independent Offices Appropriations Act (IOAA), codified at 31 U.S.C. 9701 (providing, inter alia, that “[e]ach charge shall be . . . based on . . . the costs to the Government [and] the value of the service or thing to the recipient”). However, in lieu of appropriations, Congress has directed that immigration benefits be funded with fees. See Depts. of Commerce, Justice, and State, the Judiciary, and Related Agencies Appropriations Act for 1989, Public Law 100-459, sec. 209, 102 Stat. 2186 (1988) (codified at INA sec. 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m)). However, INA sec. 344(a), 8 U.S.C. 1455, incorporates the guidance in 31 U.S.C. 9701 by reference. On October 24, 1988, Congress amended INA sec. 344(a), 8 U.S.C. 1455, by inserting the reference to 31 U.S.C. 9701. See Public Law 100-525, sec. 9(ff), 102 Stat. 2609 (1988). In 1990, without modifying section 344 of the INA, 8 U.S.C. 1455, Congress amended INA sec. 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m) and directed that the Immigration Examinations Fee Account (IEFA) fund the full costs of providing “adjudication and naturalization services,” including services provided to immigrants at no charge, see Public Law 101-515, sec. 210(d)(1) and (2), 104 Stat. 2101, 2121 (Nov. 5, 1990), thereby providing broad statutory authority to consider all factors enumerated in INA secs. 286(m) and 344, 8 U.S.C. 1356 and 1455, as well as 31 U.S.C. 9701.

13. 针对为H-1B、H-2B和L-1非移民工作者签证类别申请的某些雇主所收取的欺诈预防和侦测费由法律规定。所得收入用于与预防和侦测移民福利申请中欺诈行为相关的活动。参见《移民与国籍法》(INA)第286(v)(2)(B)条,8 U.S.C. 1356(v)(2)(B)。收入由美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)、国务院和劳工部平均分配。自2018年7月25日起,USCIS还征收并保留50美元的北马里亚纳群岛联邦欺诈费。参见48 U.S.C. 1806(a)(6)(iv)。国土安全部将《欺诈预防和侦测账户》的授权解释为提供用于欺诈预防和侦测相关活动的补充资金,而非规定只能使用该账户资金用于此目的。欺诈侦测与国家安全局(FDNS)由移民审查费账户(IEFA)和欺诈预防和侦测账户共同提供资金。
13. The Fraud Prevention and Detection fees charged to certain employers petitioning for nonimmigrant workers in the H-1B, H-2B, and L-1 visa classifications are set by statute. Revenue is used for activities related to preventing and detecting fraud in immigration benefit requests. See INA sec. 286(v)(2)(B), 8 U.S.C. 1356(v)(2)(B). Revenue is shared equally among USCIS, Department of State, and Department of Labor. Effective July 25, 2018, USCIS also collects and retains the $50 Commonwealth of the Northern Mariana Islands fraud fee. See 48 U.S.C. 1806(a)(6)(iv). DHS interprets Fraud Prevention and Detection Account authority as providing supplemental funding to cover activities related to fraud prevention and detection and not prescribing that only those funds may be used for that purpose. The Fraud Detection and National Security Directorate (FDNS) is funded out of both the IEFA and the Fraud Prevention and Detection Account.
返回引用处
Back to Citation
14. 某些H-1B费用由其他法律规定。所得收入由USCIS、劳工部(DOL)和国家科学基金会共享。USCIS获得其中5%的资金。USCIS将H-1B非移民申请人账户用作补充资金,以支付法律授权的有限H-1B申请及移民工作者申请的裁决活动。参见《移民与国籍法》第286(s)(5)条,8 U.S.C. 1356(s)(5)。H-1B非移民申请人账户并未完全为USCIS的H-1B项目提供资金。因此,USCIS还使用IEFA费用来管理该项目。对于《移民与国籍法》第286(s)(5)条(8 U.S.C. 1356(s)(5))所授权或要求的有限用途,不需要使用IEFA费用。
14. Certain H-1B fees are required by other laws. Revenue is shared among USCIS, DOL, and the National Science Foundation. USCIS receives 5 percent of these funds. USCIS uses the H-1B Nonimmigrant Petitioner Account as supplemental funding for the limited H-1B petition and petition for immigrant worker adjudication activities authorized by statute. See INA sec. 286(s)(5), 8 U.S.C. 1356(s)(5). The H-1B Nonimmigrant Petitioner Account does not fully fund the H-1B program at USCIS. As such, USCIS also uses IEFA fees to administer the program. IEFA fees are not required for those limited purposes authorized or required by INA sec. 286(s)(5), 8 U.S.C. 1356(s)(5).

22. 见OMB A-25号通告第5节(a)款和(b)款。OMB A-25号通告的主要目标是确保联邦机构收回向用户提供特定服务的全部成本及相关成本。见OMB A-25号通告,58 FR 38142, 38144。全部成本包括但不限于:(1)直接和间接人员成本,包括薪金和附加福利,如医疗保险和退休金;(2)实物间接成本、咨询费用及其他间接成本,包括材料和用品成本、公用事业费、保险费、差旅费,以及土地、建筑物和设备的租金或估算租金;(3)管理和监督成本;以及(4)执法、征收、研究以及制定标准和法规的成本。见同上,第6节,58 FR 38142, 38145。
22. See OMB Circular A-25, section 5(a) and (b). The primary objective of OMB Circular A-25 is to ensure that Federal agencies recover the full cost of providing specific services to users and associated cost. See OMB Circular A-25, 58 FR 38142, 38144. Full costs include, but are not limited to: (1) Direct and indirect personnel costs, including salaries and fringe benefits, such as medical insurance and retirement; (2) Physical overhead, consulting, and other indirect costs, including material and supply costs, utilities, insurance, travel, and rents or imputed rents on land, building, and equipment; (3) Management and supervisory costs; and (4) Cost of enforcement, collection, research, and establishment of standards and regulations. See id., section 6, 58 FR 38142, 38145.

47. 国土安全部（DHS）可以调整费用及费用计算方法，包括基于政策偏好和价值判断进行调整，只要其在法定权限范围内行事并对调整提供合理的解释。参见，例如，联邦通信委员会诉福克斯电视台案（FCC v. Fox Television Stations, Inc.），556 U.S. 502, 513-16（2009年）（“如果机构承认变更、提供合理解释并考虑任何严重的信赖利益，则该机构可以改变政策”）；机动车制造商协会诉州农场共同汽车保险公司案（Motor Vehicle Mfrs. Ass'n v. State Farm Mut. Auto. Ins. Co.），463 U.S. 29, 43（1983年）（“机构必须审查相关数据，并阐明一个令人满意的解释，表明所认定的事实与所作选择之间存在合理联系”）（内部引用省略）。
47. DHS may adjust fees and fee methodologies, including based on policy preferences and value judgments, so long as it acts within its statutory authority and provides a reasoned explanation of the adjustment. See, e.g., FCC v. Fox Television Stations, Inc., 556 U.S. 502, 513-16 (2009) (“the agency may change policy if it acknowledges the change, offers a rational explanation, and considers any serious reliance interests); Motor Vehicle Mfrs. Ass'n v. State Farm Mut. Auto. Ins. Co., 463 U.S. 29, 43 (1983) (“the agency must examine relevant data and articulate a satisfactory explanation showing a rational connection between the facts found and the choice made”) (internal quotations omitted).

Cal. 2020) (“ ILRC ”); see also Nw. Immigrant Rts. Project v. U.S. Citizenship & Immigr. Servs., 496 F. Supp. 3d 31 (D.D.C. 2020) (“ NWIRP ”). The DHS 2024 Fee Rule published on January 4, 2024, and replaced the enjoined regulation.

72. 2010年收费规则将N-400表格的费用定为595美元,但若要完全收回成本则本应将费用提高至655美元。参见75 FR 33446,33462-33463(2010年6月11日)。2016年收费规则将N-400表格的费用定为640美元,但若连同生物识别服务费一并收取以覆盖全部成本,则本应收取946美元。参见81 FR 73292,73307(2016年10月24日)(2016年收费规则)。在2024年收费规则中,该费用为760美元,但根据纸质申请评估的全部成本为1,135美元。比较8 CFR 106.2(b)(3)与USCIS、DHS,《移民审查费账户,费用审查支持文件及附录》(2023年11月),https://www.regulations.gov/​document/​USCIS-2021-0010-8176。
72. The 2010 Fee Rule established a fee of $595 for Form N-400 but would have had to raise the fee to $655 to recover the full cost. See 75 FR 33446, 33462-33463 (June 11, 2010). The 2016 Fee Rule established a fee of $640 for Form N-400, but would have had to charge, together with the biometric services fee, $946 to cover the full cost. See 81 FR 73292, 73307 (Oct. 24, 2016) (2016 Fee Rule). In the 2024 Fee Rule, the fee was $760, although the full costs assessed was $1,135 based on paper filings. Compare8 CFR 106.2(b)(3), with USCIS, DHS, “Immigration Examinations Fee Account, Fee Review Supporting Documentation with Addendum” (Nov. 2023), https://www.regulations.gov/​document/​USCIS-2021-0010-8176.
返回引文
Back to Citation
73. “提供裁决和入籍服务的费用可以设定在能够确保收回提供所有此类服务的全部成本的水平,包括免费向庇护申请人或其他移民提供的类似服务的成本。此类费用也可以设定在能够收回与所征收费用的管理相关的任何额外成本的水平。” 《移民与国籍法》(INA)第286(m)条,8 U.S.C. 1356(m)(着重号为后加)。“当然,在涉及某一机构的案件中,制定法的含义很可能是该机构被授权行使一定程度的自由裁量权。例如,有些制定法'明确授权'某一机构赋予某一特定制定法术语以含义。另一些制定法则授权某一机构制定规则以'填补'制定法方案的细节,或在某一术语或短语所施加的限制范围内进行规范,该术语或短语'给机构留有灵活性',例如'适当的'或'合理的'。” Loper Bright Enterprises诉Raimondo案,144 S. Ct. 2244, 2263(2024年)(内部引文省略);另见Paz诉Mayorkas案,767 F. Supp. 3d 368, 378(E.D. Tex. 2025年)(“第1356(m)条两次使用了'可以'一词,该词被理解为具有自由裁量的含义。”)(引用Kucana诉Holder案,558 U.S.C. 233(2010年))。
73. “[F]ees for providing adjudication and naturalization services may be set at a level that will ensure recovery of the full costs of providing all such services, including the costs of similar services provided without charge to asylum applicants or other immigrants. Such fees may also be set at a level that will recover any additional costs associated with the administration of the fees collected.” INA sec. 286(m), 8 U.S.C. 1356(m) (emphasis added). “In a case involving an agency, of course, the statute's meaning may well be that the agency is authorized to exercise a degree of discretion. For example, some statutes `expressly delegate' to an agency the authority to give meaning to a particular statutory term. Others empower an agency to prescribe rules to `fill up the details' of a statutory scheme, or to regulate subject to the limits imposed by a term or phrase that `leaves agencies with flexibility,' such as `appropriate' or `reasonable.' ” Loper Bright Enterprises v. Raimondo, 144 S. Ct. 2244, 2263 (2024) (internal citations omitted); see also Paz v. Mayorkas, 767 F. Supp. 3d 368, 378 (E.D. Tex. 2025) (“§ 1356(m) uses the word `may' twice, and this word is understood to have a discretionary import.”) (citing to Kucana v. Holder,558 U.S.C. 233 (2010)).

74. 《独立办公室拨款法》(IOAA),编入31 U.S.C. 9701,授予联邦机构征收使用费的权力。根据IOAA征收的费用通常存入美国财政部普通基金,机构不能直接使用;但是,《移民与国籍法》第344条,8 U.S.C. 1455,要求国土安全部(DHS)将费用存入移民审查与认证账户(IEFA),该账户可供美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)使用,直至用尽为止。
74. The Independent Offices Appropriations Act (IOAA) codified at 31 U.S.C. 9701, grants Federal agencies authority to assess user fees. The fees collected under the IOAA are generally deposited in the general fund of the U.S. Treasury and are not directly available to the agency; however, INA sec. 344, 8 U.S.C. 1455, requires DHS to deposit the fees in the IEFA, which is available for use by USCIS until expended.

77. 参见,例如,Madeleine Sumption与Sarah Flamm,“美国移民入籍的经济价值”,移民政策研究所(2012年9月),https://www.migrationpolicy.org/​sites/​default/​files/​publications/​citizenship-premium.pdf。然而,研究指出,入籍带来的一些表面经济利益是由其他因素造成的:“入籍移民具有更高的教育水平、更好的语言能力,以及在美国更多的工作经验。” 同上,第1页。

However, research has noted that some of the apparent economic benefits of naturalization are due to other factors: “naturalized immigrants have higher levels of education, better language skills, and more work experience in the United States.” Id. at 1.

Id. at 6206-6208. Had DHS left the income ceiling for the reduced fee Form N-400 at its prior level of 200 percent of the FPGs, this would have allowed DHS to further limit the fee increases to other forms. See id. at 6198-6204 (Table 1).

110. 国土安全部承认，外国人还必须通过提交I-90表，即《申请更换永久居民卡》，支付费用以更新其I-551表，即永久居民卡。见8 CFR 106.2(a)(1)。然而，该费用（465美元）明显低于N-400表和N-336表的拟议费用，且I-551表通常每10年需要更新一次，这使大多数合法永久居民有足够时间为申请费存钱。见美国公民及移民服务局（USCIS）、国土安全部，《USCIS政策手册》，第11卷，旅行和身份证件，B部分，永久居民卡，第1章，目的和背景，https://www.uscis.gov/​policy-manual/​volume-11-part-b-chapter-1（最后更新于2026年2月3日）。
110. DHS acknowledges that aliens must also pay a fee to renew their Form I-551, Permanent Resident Card, by filing Form I-90, Application to Replace Permanent Resident Card. See 8 CFR 106.2(a)(1). However, that fee ($465) is significantly lower than the proposed fees for Form N-400 and Form N-336, and Form I-551 typically must be renewed every 10 years, which gives most LPRs time to save up for the filing fee. See USCIS, DHS, “USCIS Policy Manual,” Volume 11, Travel and Identity Documents, Part B, Permanent Resident Cards, Chp. 1, Purpose and Background, https://www.uscis.gov/​policy-manual/​volume-11-part-b-chapter-1 (last updated Feb. 3, 2026).

119. I-912表，即费用豁免申请，必须与N-400表或N-336表一同提交。I-912表在N-400表或N-336表被裁定之前先行裁定。若I-912表获批准，美国公民及移民服务局将为N-400表或N-336表签发收据通知，并继续裁定N-400表或N-336表。若I-912表被拒绝，整个申请材料包将退还给申请人。见美国公民及移民服务局，I-912表，《费用豁免请求说明》，https://www.uscis.gov/​sites/​default/​files/​document/​forms/​i-912instr.pdf（2025年7月22日版）。
119. Form I-912, or a fee waiver request, must be submitted together with Form N-400 or with Form N-336. Form I-912 is adjudicated before the Form N-400 or Form N-336 is adjudicated. If the Form I-912 is approved, USCIS will issue a receipt notice for the Form N-400 or Form N-336 and proceed with adjudicating Form N-400 or Form N-336. If the Form I-912 is denied, the entire application package will be returned to the applicant. See USCIS, Form I-912, “Instructions for Request for Fee Waiver,” https://www.uscis.gov/​sites/​default/​files/​document/​forms/​i-912instr.pdf (July 22, 2025 ed.).

121. 如下文所述，任何超过750万美元年度限额（10 U.S.C. 1790）的成本，将由其他非军方N-400表外国申请人以更高费用的形式承担。在2024财年，与军人入籍相关的费用约为2000万美元。因此，这些资金中约38%由国防部（DOW）报销（750万美元 ÷ 2000万美元 = 38%（四舍五入））。
121. As discussed later, any costs that exceed the $7.5 million annual limit (10 U.S.C. 1790) would be paid for by other non-military Form N-400 alien applicants in the form of higher fees. In FY 2024, fees related to naturalization for aliens in the military cost around $20 million. Thus, about 38 percent of these funds are reimbursed by DOW ($7.5 million ÷ $20 million = 38 percent (rounded)).

152. 本分析中的人口估计数仅基于已批准的费用豁免申请。该数据不包括所有费用豁免申请或被拒绝的申请。因此,总体人口可能被低估。国土安全部(DHS)认识到,申请费用豁免但被拒绝的外国人也将受到拟议取消费用豁免的影响。这些个人在新政策下可能会节省时间,但国土安全部没有关于他们的数据。部分外国人可能在审阅I-912表格后自行判断其不符合费用豁免资格。这些申请人未填写I-912表格。他们也可能节省时间,但国土安全部无法估计此类人数。
152. The population estimates in this analysis are based only on approved fee waiver requests. The data does not include all fee waiver requests or requests that were denied. Therefore, the total population may be underestimated. DHS recognizes that aliens who requested a fee waiver but were denied would also be affected by the proposed removal of fee waivers. These individuals may save time under the new policy, but DHS does not have data about them. Some aliens may review Form I-912 and decide on their own that they do not qualify for a fee waiver. These applicants do not complete Form I-912. They may also save time, but DHS cannot estimate how many people this would affect.

165. 时间机会成本的估算仅针对已批准的申请,因为所提供的人口数据不包括费用豁免请求总数,也不包括可能被拒绝的任何请求。因此,本分析中的时间机会成本可能被低估。
165. The estimates for opportunity cost of time are for approvals only because the population data provided does not include total fee waiver requests and is not inclusive of any requests that may have been denied. Therefore, the opportunity cost of time may be underestimated in this analysis.

169. 本分析中的人口估算仅基于已批准的费用减免请求。该数据并未涵盖全部费用减免请求,也不包括被拒绝的请求。因此,总体人口数量可能被低估。美国国土安全部(DHS)理解,申请减费但被拒绝的人员也会受到拟议取消减费政策的影响。这些人员在新政策下可能节省时间。但是,美国国土安全部没有关于这些人员的数据。此外,一些外国人可能在查阅表格N-400第10部分后,自行认定其不符合减费资格。这些申请人不会填写第10部分。他们也可能节省时间,但美国国土安全部无法估算这将影响多少人。
169. The population estimates in this analysis are based only on approved fee reduction requests. The data does not include all fee reduction requests, nor does it include requests that were denied. As a result, the total population may be underestimated. DHS understands that people who requested reduced fees but were denied would also be affected by the proposed removal of reduced fees. These individuals may save time under the new policy. However, DHS does not have data about these individuals. Additionally, some aliens may review Part 10 of Form N-400 and decide themselves that they do not qualify for a reduced fee. These applicants do not complete Part 10. They may also save time, but DHS cannot estimate how many people this would affect.

170. The median accounts for the variability between the populations before and after the 2024 Fee Rule went into effect as there were noticeable changes in filing behavior between the two periods even though data are currently limited for the time period after the 2024 Fee Rule went into effect.

172. 美国公民及移民服务局(USCIS)估计,N-400表申请人阅读说明并填写当前第10部分的时间负担约为15分钟(0.25小时)。由于减费申请人群体相对较小(约占总体人口的3%),美国公民及移民服务局不会调整其余未受影响的97%人群的时间负担。
172. USCIS estimates the time burden for a N-400 respondent to read the instructions and complete the current Part 10 of the form is about 15 minutes (0.25 hours). Because the respondent population for reduced fees is relatively small (approximately 3 percent of the overall population), USCIS would not modify the time burden for the other unaffected 97 percent.

189. 见美国劳工统计局(BLS),《所有城市消费者消费价格指数历史数据(CPI-U):美国城市平均值,所有项目,按月》,https://data.bls.gov/​pdq/​SurveyOutputServlet(最后访问日期:2026年2月3日)。通货膨胀计算方法:(1)计算基准年(1995年)和当前年(2025年)的月平均CPI-U;(2)用当前年CPI-U减去基准年CPI-U;(3)将基准年CPI-U与当前年CPI-U之差除以基准年CPI-U;(4)乘以100 = [(2025年月平均CPI-U−1995年月平均CPI-U)÷(1995年月平均CPI-U)]×100 = [(324.054−152.383)÷152.383] = (171.671÷152.383) = 1.126×100 = 112.6% = 113%(四舍五入)。通货膨胀调整值计算方法:1995年的1亿美元×2.13 = 2025年的2.13亿美元。
189. See BLS, “Historical Consumer Price Index for All Urban Consumers (CPI-U): U.S. city average, all items, by month,” https://data.bls.gov/​pdq/​SurveyOutputServlet (last visited Feb. 3, 2026). Calculation of inflation: (1) Calculate the average monthly CPI-U for the reference year (1995) and the current year (2025); (2) Subtract reference year CPI-U from current year CPI-U; (3) Divide the difference of the reference year CPI-U and current year CPI-U by the reference year CPI-U; (4) Multiply by 100 = [(Average monthly CPI-U for 2025−Average monthly CPI-U for 1995) ÷ (Average monthly CPI-U for 1995)] × 100 = [(324.054−152.383) ÷ 152.383] = (171.671 ÷ 152.383) = 1.126 × 100 = 112.6 percent = 113 percent (rounded). Calculation of inflation-adjusted value: $100 million in 1995 dollars × 2.13 = $213 million in 2025 dollars.
